But current render_rows doesn't have that parameter.\n\n4. `test_export_appends_totals_row`: asserts export_csv([[1, 2], [3, 4]], totals=True) == \"1,2\\n3,4\\nTOTAL,4,6\". The test `test_export_appends_totals_row` expects \"TOTAL,4,6\" where 4 and 6 are the sum of columns? Actually rows are [[1,2],[3,4]]. Column sums are [1+3=4, 2+4=6]. So the totals row is \"TOTAL,4,6\".\n\nThe test `test_render_marks_last_row_as_total` expects [\"1,2\", \"TOTAL,3,4\"] which is different: it marks the last row as total with the values of the last row, not the sum of all rows.
